### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP は「届いたことを確かめながら、順番どおり確実に届ける」通信方式で、UDP は「確認せず、速く送りっぱなしにする」通信方式です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] **Rebase** para actualizar tu rama local, todavía no compartida, con los últimos cambios de la base y dejar el historial lineal. **Merge** cuando la rama ya está publicada o la usan otras personas, porque reescribir historia compartida causa problemas a quien tenga copias.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、可靠传输的：先握手建立连接，保证数据不丢、不乱序、不重复；UDP 是无连接、不保证可靠的，发出去就不管了，但开销小、延迟低。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own local feature branch before it's shared, to keep the history linear and clean. Merge once the branch is shared or public, since rebasing published history breaks other people's copies.
```

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a database on disk before the change is applied to the data files. If the system crashes mid-write, it replays that log on restart to recover committed work and discard anything half-finished.

- 找出 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把锁的范围缩小，只在真正需要的地方加锁。
- 改动留在这个任务已有的分支上，提交到该分支。
- 跑一遍相关测试，把结果（测试命令和通过情况）一并报告。
- 说明改了哪几处锁，以及改前改后的差别。

不要做的：
- 不合并，不把草稿 PR 标记为可合并。
- 不改 review 之外的功能。

地点：沿用这个任务原来的仓库和分支。
```

const fmt = n => '¥' + Number(n).toLocaleString('zh-CN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2});
let list = [];
let openId = null;

function render() {

}

function load(v) {
  list = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  render();
}
function save() {
  ducter.set('expenses', list);
}

$('add').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const day = $('fDay').value.trim();
  const what = $('fWhat').value.trim();
  const amount = parseFloat($('fAmt').value);
  if (!day || !what || !(amount > 0)) return;
  list.push({ id: Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6), day, what, amount: Math.round(amount * 100) / 100 });
  $('fWhat').value = '';
  $('fAmt').value = '';
  $('add').closest('details').open = false;
  save();
  render();
});

$('days').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
  if (del) {
    list = list.filter(x => x.id !== del.dataset.del);
    openId = null;
    save();
    render();
    return;
  }
  const row = e.target.closest('.k-row[data-id]');
  if (row) {
    openId = openId === row.dataset.id ? null : row.dataset.id;
    render();
  }
});

ducter.onChange(() => { Promise.resolve(ducter.get('expenses')).then(load); });
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('expenses')).then(load);
$('fDay').value = '2026-11-28';
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

    ducter.summary(left + " 个还在等 / " + left + " waiting");
  }

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get("landed")).then(function (v) {
    landed = v || {};
    render();
  });
  ducter.onChange(function (v) {
    landed = v || {};
    render();
  });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"wey-gu/ducter#412","sub":"Compaction cut · waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","sub":"Retry on 502 · CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","sub":"Flaky compaction test · waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"wey-gu/ducter#418","sub":"Apps bar · still a draft"}]}

在现有 TTL 测试的基础上，再加一条测试：用假时钟把时间推进到恰好等于 TTL 的那一刻，确认此时值已经过期（或按现有语义确认边界行为），并且在到期前一刻仍然有效。

完成标准：
- 新测试加在现有 TTL 测试文件里，使用假时钟，不依赖真实计时。
- 全部测试通过，并在报告里给出测试输出。
- 连续跑几次确认不会时好时坏。

范围之外：不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR。工作留在现有分支 ducter/foo-ttl 上提交即可。
地点：~/dev/foo。
